Liquid original source qualification

P10b4c3 · 2026-10-11T08:55:52.321Z · v22.12.0 · linux/x64. Ten immutable original Shopify Dawn files, byte counts/SHA-256 pinned to one original commit. Reviewed names, kinds, source lines and dependencies are independent of production facts.

12/12 original StickyHeader declarations · 0 misses · 0 extras
42/42 reviewed source dependency sites, including two unsupported app-block render sites and two section-group sites. Original delimiter inventory: 1626/1626 matches, 0 misses, 0 extras. Recovery and engine gaps remain explicit.

One canonical original template identity per file, twelve exact UTF-16 declaration/ownership/header proofs and ten actual syntax-cache hits preserve cold/warm/revision graphs. Only two dependencies have eligible indexed targets in this deliberately partial portfolio; both resolve with original syntax and same-application filesystem evidence. Missing targets do not trigger disk search or dependency installation. Original source text is parsed; no Liquid engine, target app, theme compiler, rendered output or generated bundle executes.

The supported profile retains native tags, output, controls, comments, raw content, multiline Liquid tags, variable binder metadata and Shopify body regions. Raw bodies use original native delimiter hulls because scanner content offsets can be empty. Shopify JavaScript bodies preserve their literal original text, class/constructor/method/function/binding source, independent of unrelated native recovery. Interpolated style output, app-block render syntax, a multiline loop modifier, custom tags, ambiguous native literal opening markers, recovered owners, nested raw-engine disagreement, doc-body grammar recovery, external/data/typed/control-dependent/interpolated scripts, non-HTML asset processors and unknown engines remain gaps. Assignment variables are metadata; no template functions, generated render helpers or virtual graph files are invented.

Shopify's original theme directory contract qualifies one indexed/configured shopify-theme boundary. Explicit original quoted render/include/section/layout dependency sites map to original snippets/sections/layout files. Paths, app blocks, JSON section-group expansion, implicit layouts, dynamic filenames, Jekyll include/include_relative, scope/parameter binding, arbitrary engine lookup and rendered/runtime equivalence remain unqualified. Shopify JavaScript is not Liquid-rendered; section/block/snippet location evidence is required. Raw and doc body boundaries protect literal/non-rendered source. This narrow single-theme portfolio does not establish broad Liquid or framework accuracy.

Pinned community Liquid grammar 0.1.0, ABI 14, 136,087-byte WASM, SHA-256 b5c08a0d17c21457141647595ffee1c028dd31179557e6472bd6e20b08ae37a8. Two isolated generated-C/scanner builds reproduce identical bytes with checksum-pinned CLI 0.26.9/WASI SDK 29.0/container tools. The original MIT notice ships. Twenty assets verify offline; ordinary build/index needs neither downloads nor upstream npm hooks/grammar-generation JavaScript.

Fresh direct parser observations

Immutable original Dawn price source regions
Three fresh processes: median runtime initialization 9.1 ms; Liquid/HTML grammar-and-query loads 5.1/21.6 ms; native extraction 20.0 ms; JavaScript load/extraction 0.0/0.0 ms; combined first extraction excluding loads 20.0 ms; Linux process-image peak 107.5 MiB.

Immutable original Dawn StickyHeader JavaScript body
Three fresh processes: median runtime initialization 9.2 ms; Liquid/HTML grammar-and-query loads 5.4/21.5 ms; native extraction 35.4 ms; JavaScript load/extraction 636.6/20.8 ms; combined first extraction excluding loads 56.0 ms; Linux process-image peak 245.1 MiB.

Linux /proc VmHWM covers one process image since exec; IPC/process-tree, mixed-history and native Windows/macOS remain full P10 release gates. Other project validation is idle during measurement. Existing-stack guardrails use the separate five-repeat comparison against 0423d2f. Conformance and production-package gates are recorded in the roadmap.

Implementation digest (275 source/asset/pin/build/audit/memory/package/conformance files): 36172886b7343849bab9999020bdea5eab2bbb946402d46a4e17e1866f19dba1. Reproduce node --experimental-sqlite --import tsx scripts/qualify-liquid-source.mjs --output docs/liquid-source-accuracy.html; --source DIRECTORY verifies these immutable paths offline.
